\n # Roadmap\n\nPlans for the next releases. Items move to the changelog when they ship.\n\n## 1.0\n\n- Scheduler loop with persistent next-run times\n- TOML job table\n- Command-line listing and run-once\n- Persistent state file with atomic writes\n- Structured logging\n\n## 1.1\n\n- Notification hooks\n- Run metrics\n- Daemon mode\n\n## Retry policy\n\n- Retry failed runs with exponential backoff\n- Per-job attempt limit from the job table\n- Jitter so that retries of many jobs do not align\n- Tests with a fake clock\n\n## Review notes\n\nReviewed weekly. The rebase records\n# whoever runs this script as the committer of the rebased commits and keeps\n# each commit's author date, time zone included, as its committer date. The\n# rebased branch is force-pushed with a lease, so a push nobody has seen is\n# never overwritten. Commit ids are omitted from the patches (the From line\n# of every patch is all zeros): the archive records content, not history\n# positions.\nset -eu\ncd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/..\"\n\nname=$1\nbranch=$2\ndir=\"docs/patches/$name\"\nstart=$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)\nonto=$(git rev-parse HEAD)\n\ngit rebase -q --committer-date-is-author-date \"$onto\" \"$branch\"\ngit checkout -q \"$start\"\ngit push -q --force-with-lease origin \"$branch\"\n\nrm -rf \"$dir\"\nmkdir -p \"$dir\"\ngit format-patch -q --no-thread --zero-commit --no-signature -o \"$dir\" \"$onto..$branch\"\n{\n    printf '# Series %s: exported from the tip of %s; the patches in order.\\n' \"$name\" \"$branch\"\n    ls \"$dir\" | grep '\\.patch$'\n} > \"$dir/series\"\ngit add \"$dir\"\ngit commit -q -m \"Archive the $name series\"\necho \"archived $(grep -c '\\.patch$' \"$dir/series\") patches of $branch in $dir\"\n\n\nFILE tools/gen_api_docs.py \n #!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Generate docs/API.md from the docstrings of the orbit package.\n\nThe output depends only on orbit/*.py, so the file can be regenerated at any\ncommit. tools/release.sh runs it before every release.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport ast\nimport pathlib\n\nROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\n\n\ndef signature(fn):\n    args = fn.args\n    names = [a.arg for a in args.posonlyargs + args.args]\n    defaults = [None] * (len(names) - len(args.defaults)) + [ast.unparse(d) for d in args.defaults]\n    parts = [n if d is None else f\"{n}={d}\" for n, d in zip(names, defaults)]\n    if args.vararg:\n        parts.append(\"*\" + args.vararg.arg)\n    for kw, d in zip(args.kwonlyargs, args.kw_defaults):\n        parts.append(kw.arg if d is None else f\"{kw.arg}={ast.unparse(d)}\")\n    if args.kwarg:\n        parts.append(\"**\" + args.kwarg.arg)\n    return \", \".join(parts)\n\n\ndef describe(node, level):\n    doc = ast.get_docstring(node) or \"Undocumented.\"\n    if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):\n        lines = [f\"{'#' * level} class {node.name}\", \"\", doc, \"\"]\n        for child in node.body:\n            if isinstance(child, ast.FunctionDef) and not child.name.startswith(\"_\"):\n                lines += describe(child, level + 1)\n        return lines\n    return [f\"{'#' * level} {node.name}({signature(node)})\", \"\", doc, \"\"]\n\n\ndef main():\n    out = [\"# orbit API\", \"\", \"Generated by tools/gen_api_docs.py from the package docstrings; do not edit.\", \"\"]\n    for path in sorted((ROOT / \"orbit\").glob(\"*.py\")):\n        if path.stem == \"__main__\":\n            continue\n        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text())\n        module = \"orbit\" if path.stem == \"__init__\" else f\"orbit.{path.stem}\"\n        out += [f\"## {module}\", \"\", ast.get_docstring(tree) or \"Undocumented.\", \"\"]\n        for node in tree.body:\n            if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)) and not node.name.startswith(\"_\"):\n                out += describe(node, 3)\n    (ROOT / \"docs\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n    (ROOT / \"docs\" / \"API.md\").write_text(\"\\n\".join(out).rstrip(\"\\n\") + \"\\n\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n\n\nFILE tools/release.sh \n #!/bin/sh\n# tools/release.sh: cut a release, or refresh VERSION for a dist build.\n#\n#   tools/release.sh          write VERSION from git describe (used by make dist)\n#   tools/release.sh 1.4.0    release: VERSION, docs/API.md, CHANGELOG.md, commit, tag\n#\n# x.y.0 releases get an annotated tag; point releases get a lightweight\n# tag. SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH fixes the time written into the changelog heading\n# and the date of the tag (reproducible builds); unset, it is the committer\n# time of the commit the release is cut from.\nset -eu\ncd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/..\"\n\nif [ $# -eq 0 ]; then\n    git describe --tags --always > VERSION\n    exit 0\nfi\n\nver=\"v$1\"\nif [ -z \"${SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH:-}\" ]; then\n    SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH=$(git log -1 --format=%ct)\nfi\nstamp=$(date -u -d \"@$SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH\" '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S +0000')\nprev=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 2>/dev/null || true)\n\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$ver\" > VERSION\npython3 tools/gen_api_docs.py\n{\n    printf '\\n## %s - %s\\n\\n' \"$ver\" \"$stamp\"\n    if [ -n \"$prev\" ]; then\n        git log --no-merges --format='- %s' \"$prev..HEAD\"\n    else\n        git log --no-merges --format='- %s'\n    fi\n} >> CHANGELOG.md\n\ngit add VERSION docs/API.md CHANGELOG.md\ngit commit -q -m \"Release $ver\"\n\n# The tag is made in the name of the release commit's committer and dated\n# from SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH, the time in the changelog heading, so the tag and\n# the notes agree whoever runs the script and whenever the commit is made.\nRELEASE_NAME=$(git log -1 --format=%cn)\nRELEASE_EMAIL=$(git log -1 --format=%ce)\nRELEASE_DATE=\"@$SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH +0000\"\ncase \"$ver\" in\n    *.0)\n        GIT_COMMITTER_NAME=\"$RELEASE_NAME\" GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL=\"$RELEASE_EMAIL\" \\\n            GIT_COMMITTER_DATE=\"$RELEASE_DATE\" git tag -a -m \"orbit $ver\" \"$ver\"\n        ;;\n    *)\n        git tag \"$ver\"\n        ;;\nesac\necho \"released $ver\"\n\n\nFILE docs/patches/cache-warmup/series \n # Series cache-warmup: exported from the tip of feature/cache-warmup; the patches in order.\n0001-Warm-the-cache-in-a-background-thread.patch\n0002-Add-a-cache-size-limit.patch\n\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
